# Aurora Viewer - archived tasks

Finished tasks moved out of [TASKS.md](../TASKS.md) by
`scripts/archive-tasks.ps1`, under the same headings, most recently archived
first. Search this file when you need the history of a feature; open work
is in TASKS.md.

## General

- ✅ LiveMesh installation, usability and responsiveness (human requests, 2026-10-29; PR #1511; task `livemesh-manual-discovery`; validated by the human in the supplied test viewer, merge authorized on 2026-10-29): plug-in 0.14.6 defaults automatic updates to 2 seconds, preserving saved intervals. Blender discovery is explicit; selecting one executable installs directly with progress and completion feedback. Connection and Plugins tabs use readable scoped text. Complete missing skin weights in the exported copy, isolate failed sources and show per-object warning dialogs with copyable errors. Negotiate packed f32 geometry and worker-side lossless compression with 64 MiB wire and 256 MiB decoded bounds; older peers and import/LOD remain supported. Bulk geometry/weight capture, eligible direct rest-mesh reads, material-only geometry reuse, a 150 ms editing pause and change-only sidebar redraws reduce Blender stalls. Aurora-specific controls/protocol; nearest-weight completion follows mesh import. Full snapshots remain the update model.
  - Verification: Blender 2.93/5.2 pass 229 native tests with one version-specific skip, both installation workflows and 18 client tests. Native selected installation, in-process upgrades, source failures, warning dialogs, tabs, scoped text and real offline transfers are verified. EN/FR/JA catalogs, architecture and setup/testing guides are updated. Blender 5.2.1 installation and activation match the rebuilt viewer's exact bundled sources.
  - Reported-source measurements: 104,816 triangles, 252,496 exported corners; ordinary JSON 69.51 MiB becomes 18.30 MiB packed JSON and 7.43 MiB transferred. The real offline initial main-thread callback improves from 1.86 s to 0.31 s; repeated editing triggers no capture during the test gesture (4.6 ms maximum callback), then a 0.375 s capture after the pause, versus old pauses up to 2.33 s. Worker preparation is about 0.49 s; exported geometry and the saved source file remain unchanged. Final full workspace checks pass 6127 tests, zero failures and 30 ignored; PowerShell syntax, formatting and Clippy are clean. Final Connection/Plugins/linked-object demo runs exit zero and their captures are inspected.

- ✅ Worn objects trailing behind their walking wearer (tester report, 2026-10-28; task `attachment-drift`; no matching open issue; validated by the human on the grid on 2026-10-28, the wearer walking and typing): a cigarette holder worn on the mouth of a mesh head floated in the air, following its wearer, while the wearer walked and typed, and went back between the lips once the wearer stopped. Typing starts the prop's script, which sends terse updates of a child prim of the worn linkset every second or so; each one starts a correction (the fade of `world/motion.rs`), during which the sync places the prim at each frame while its root stays on its GPU anchor. When the correction faded, the prim was only noted as touched: the sync skipped it (nothing to rebuild) and its wearer's followers stop at the anchored root, so its record kept the world matrix of the last fading frame, behind the walking wearer, until the next update or detail check. `ObjectStore::expire_corrections` now marks the records to place again: the prim goes back on its anchor. Diagnosed on the grid with temporary placement logs (the CPU place was right, the prim alternated between the sync and its anchor). Also, for Legacy Viewer parity, a worn prim no longer moves with the velocity or acceleration of its update, only spins (LLViewerObject::idleUpdate returns before interpolateLinearMotion for LLVOVolume::isAttachment); no such velocity was seen in this case. Unit tests `a_faded_correction_puts_a_worn_prim_back_on_its_anchor`, `a_worn_prim_spins_but_never_drifts_with_a_reported_velocity`; full `scripts/check.ps1` green.
- ✅ Tint friends' name plates (human request, 2026-10-28; task `friend-plate-tint`; no matching open issue; validated by the human in demo on 2026-10-28): a friend's plate surface takes half of the friends' color of Preferences › Avatars › Name colors, with a border of that color, read each frame so a new color shows at once (a lighter tint did not show enough in the human's test). A contact set's background replaces both outright (no mix), a set's border replaces the border, and a blocked friend keeps the plain surface. Text contrast is kept like the set tints. New switch Preferences › Avatars › Aurora avatar plates › "Tint friends' plates", on by default; the Friends color row stays editable while it is on, even with names colored by relation off. Aurora's own, no Legacy Viewer counterpart (its classic tags only color the name).
  - "Color the names by relation" is on by default again (it was off since 2026-10-14), as the Legacy Viewer always colors them: friends' names in the friends' color. Settings version 27 turns it on in earlier files, which all wrote the former default.
  - Full `scripts/check.ps1` green (scripts, formatting, Clippy, workspace tests). Demo: `AURORA_DEMO_TAGS=voice` ('Orion le Grand' is the friend).

- ✅ Saved outfits cannot be deleted ([#1293](https://github.com/Aurora-Viewer/Aurora-Viewer/issues/1293); task `outfit-delete`, 2026-10-28; validated by the human on the grid on 2026-10-28): "Delete outfit" in the Appearance gallery failed with "The server rejected the operation (HTTP 400)". It sent the move to the Trash as an AIS `PATCH /category/<id>` with a `parent_id`, which AIS refuses; the Legacy Viewer moves the folder with MoveInventoryFolder (LLInventoryModel::removeCategory → changeCategoryParent → LLViewerInventoryCategory::updateParentOnServer), as Aurora already does for outfit groups. `outfits/categories.rs` now sends that reliable UDP message and reads the old parent and the Trash back, retrying the read (not the move) until the move shows. Rename, favorite and image keep their AIS PATCH.
  - Full `scripts/check.ps1` green (6,006 passed, zero failures, 30 ignored). Validated by the human on the grid: a saved outfit that is not worn is deleted, with no HTTP 400.

- ✅ Crash watcher refused its own session in the offline demo (reported on 2026-10-14, `Error: invalid crash session directory` as the first console line): the helper process compared the canonical path of the session folder with the canonical crash root. A viewer started from a packaged (MSIX) application has the new folders of its data directory stored in the package's cache while the root keeps its ordinary location, so the two never matched: no watcher, no crash window, and one folder left per run. `--crash-watch` / `--crash-show` now take the session UUID and open only the real folder of that name under the helper's own root; in demo the root is handed over as an absolute path. A session that closed normally without a watcher is removed at the next launch. Tests: viewer / helper agreement on the session folder (demo root, relative root, working directory), refusal of paths, files and links, cleanup and single report of leftover sessions. `AURORA_DEMO_CRASH` scenarios (panic, native, missing, normal, recovery) and a killed demo viewer verified with and without `AURORA_DEMO_CRASH_DIR`, crash window captures examined; on the PC of the report the first launch removed the 1,974 folders left by the defect. `check.ps1` green: 2,014 tests passed, 24 ignored. PR authorised by the human on 2026-10-14 after the report of these runs.

  - `check.ps1` green on V6: 950 tests passed, 12 ignored; after integration of the notecards then of the visible permissions from main for the PR, 1,058 tests passed and 13 ignored, Clippy with no warning. Tests of commands / permissions / Unicode / undo, preferences, bounded files and external arguments, LSL reference, transformations / macros / includes, asynchronous save then reopening of the original, failure without sending and debug routing. FR / EN screenshots examined: editor, object, search, preferences, help, preprocessed code, preprocessing error, tabs, preprocessed save and reopening in the inventory and the object. Indicative comparison on the same 17-line script, two successive runs without a build: average UI time from 0.77 to 0.87 ms over the last four seconds. Demonstrations described in `docs/TESTING.md`. Human validation of V6 received on 2026-10-11 after the launch of the online test; the native dialogs and the external editor are not automated.
  - CI: the windowless egui test benches explicitly free their texture updates, like the notecard tests. The first pass through the merge queue revealed this requirement, enabled by the CI assertions; fix limited to the tests, validated viewer code unchanged. `check.ps1` is green again (1,056 passed, 13 ignored).
- ✅ Tab stops of the script editor (feedback of 2026-10-11): Tab inserts spaces up to the next multiple of four columns; Backspace deletes the spaces back to the previous stop, including an incomplete indent. Same behaviour in the inventory and in objects. Rules of `LLTextEditor::handleSpecialKey` and `removeCharOrTab`: do not delete code to reach a stop; an imported literal tab stays one unit. The new indents after a brace also use spaces up to the next stop.
  - Eight real egui tests cover complete / incomplete indents, Unicode / CRLF, selections, successive events within one frame, Undo / Redo and exact paste. `check.ps1` green in series: 927 tests passed, 12 ignored, Clippy with no warning. An existing timing test of the render engine failed during the first run; it passed again alone, then with the full suite, without any change to the engine.
  - Screenshots examined: `tabstops`, `object-tabstops`, `object-indent` and `syntax`. The scenario starts from seven spaces, goes back to four with Backspace, then to eight with Tab; Enter keeps this indent. Indicative before / after comparison on the same 17-line script, over two successive runs: average UI time from 0.72 ms to 0.83 ms over the last four seconds. Human validation of V6 received on 2026-10-11.
- ✅ Feedback on the script editor of 2026-10-11: Enter repeats the indent and adds a level after an opening brace in the inventory and in objects; cursor, replacement of a selection and Undo / Redo covered by the real egui tests, paste intact. Rule of `LLTextEditor::autoIndent`; Aurora improvement: also preserve literal tabs and accept spaces after the brace.
  - Fix for saving without an experience, reported on Agni: an absent / undefined / empty GetMetadata field means "None". Like `LLLiveLSLEditor`, the association is loaded on opening and sent from the editor, with no blocking metadata request at save time. "Experience" selector in object scripts: "None", experiences allowed by GetCreatorExperiences and names by GetExperienceInfo; an existing inaccessible association stays visible and kept. Aurora choice: a single selector to choose or remove the association; if a real error prevents reading it, ask for an explicit choice or a new read to avoid removing it silently. Changing only the experience is enough to enable Save and the close check. No selector in the personal inventory, like Firestorm.
  - `check.ps1` green in series: 922 tests passed, 12 ignored, Clippy with no warning. Local HTTP tests of the absence of an experience and of keeping an inaccessible association; late responses and disconnection covered. French / English screenshots examined: save without an experience then reopening, association saved then reopening, indentation with spaces / tabs, highlighting unchanged. Demo: `indent`, `object-indent`, `experience`, `experience-save`. Indicative comparison on the same 17-line script: average UI time from 0.86 ms before to 0.76 ms after (last four seconds), with no measured increase. Human validation of V6 received on 2026-10-11.
- ✅ LSL syntax highlighting in the script editor of the inventory and of objects: types, variables, native / custom functions, events, keywords, constants, numbers, strings and comments; Firestorm catalogue, Aurora palette and per-document cache, without altering the source. Six tests cover the categories, case, calls with comments, labels / states, escapes, incomplete texts, Unicode / CRLF, numbers and theme / font changes. `check.ps1` green (913 tests passed, 12 ignored). Screenshots of the inventory, objects, read-only and reopening after saving examined; demos `syntax`, `object-syntax`, `syntax-readonly`. Indicative `AURORA_PROFILE=1` comparison on the same 17-line script: average UI time from 0.84 ms before to 0.86 ms with highlighting (last four seconds). Three existing timing tests of the render engine failed during a parallel rerun; the 82 tests of the engine, then the full suite, passed again in series without any change to the engine. Human validation of V6 received on 2026-10-11.
- ✅ Editing and saving of scripts in the inventory and in objects: shared editor, opening by double-click / menu, reading with a rights check, saving through UpdateScriptAgent / UpdateScriptTask, Mono / LSL compilation and simulator diagnostics, run / stop / reset in objects. Protection of changes on closing and keeping of the source after disconnection. Demo `AURORA_DEMO_SCRIPTS=inventory|object|both|save|object-save|error|readonly`; tests of reordered UDP transfers, permission context, HTTP protocol / Unicode / experience and late responses. Feedback on Agni of 2026-10-11 fixed: a null asset UUID wrongly triggered the local template; the source is now always requested from the simulator through the inventory item / object, without marking the loaded source as modified. Masked UUID kept null in the LLSD content reader. Test reproducing the defect before the fix then passing, reopening in demo examined for inventory and object. `check.ps1` green (907 tests passed, 12 ignored); human validation of V6 received on 2026-10-11.
- ✅ **Collapse a window to its title bar by double-click** (requested on 2026-10-11): as in Firestorm, a double-click on the title bar of an interface window collapses it into a simple title bar, still movable. Another double-click restores it, with its contents and dimensions kept.
  - Shared widget: the title buttons and the contents are excluded from the gesture, the "−" button stays usable, state and dimensions are kept in the saved layout. Aurora choice: restore at the current position of the bar, rather than at Firestorm's previous expanded rectangle. Tests of the gestures, sizes, moving, serialisation and handles; before / after screenshots examined. Pre-validation of the merge and autonomous work requested by the human on 2026-10-11.
- ✅ **"Report a bug" button during the alpha test** (requested on 2026-10-10; validated by the human on 2026-10-11): a tester prepares a report from the viewer, and the developer and their agent receive the chosen information to understand the problem.
  - **Visible but clean button**: in the top bar (Phosphor `bug` icon, Aurora palette), also in the Help menu and on the login screen (it must be possible to report a login bug). Setting to remove it from the bar; at the end of the alpha, it stays only in Help.
  - **Principle, decided with the human on 2026-10-10**: the repository's issues are public, so nothing sensitive is attached automatically. The window warns that the report will be public, shows exactly what is going to be sent, and the tester unticks what they do not want to give.
  - **Report window**: banner "This report will be public on GitHub"; title and description of the bug (what I was doing, what I expected, what happened); list of the information with a checkbox and the exact value of each in the Data tab; form in a single column, with no separate summary panel (human choice of 2026-10-11).
  - **Ticked by default, can be unticked**:
    - viewer version: number, commit, build profile (see the "A single version number" line);
    - place: the tester's SLURL at the time of the report (region and position of their avatar);
    - system: OS, processor, memory, graphics card, driver, VRAM, resolution, graphics settings, fps and frame time, language, session duration.
  - **Offered, never attached automatically**:
    - screenshot: "Take a picture" (the scene, with or without the interface) and "Attach an image" buttons, preview, removal possible; reminder that it will be public and can show the chat, names and the place;
    - log excerpt (latest warnings and errors, last panic or GPU validation error), unticked by default, filtered (web addresses, identifiers, names, personal paths) and shown as is before sending. Never the whole log: it names regions, objects and avatars, and a network error message could contain an address that must not get out (capability URL; to be checked during the task);
    - avatar name.
  - **Never in the public issue**: password, token, session identifier, capability URL, chat and IM content, full log. The full report is kept locally. Human choice of 2026-10-11: ZIP of the logs offered separately, unticked by default, stored in the private Cellar and accessible only to authorised developers in the private repository.
  - Issue text in fixed sections and in English, so that an agent reads it without guessing.
  - **Human choice of 2026-10-11: A and B, at the tester's choice**:
    - Optional browser: prefilled link to "new issue", like Firestorm's `handle_report_bug` (`indra/newview/llviewermenu.cpp`). If the text exceeds the size the link allows, it is kept whole in the clipboard, to paste into GitHub.
    - Direct sending by default, offered in first position (human choice of 2026-10-11): GitHub App and Device Flow, public Client ID built in. The tester authorises the application in their browser then clicks Send; authorisation in memory for the session only, cancellation, expiry and refusal handled. No automatic retry after a lost response.
    - Both modes require a GitHub account. Direct sending: images hosted by the Aurora PHP API / Cellar and displayed automatically in the issue; optional private ZIP with the developers' GitHub login. Browser: image saved in `public`, to be dragged manually into the issue. Without an account, a local copy is possible. The issue is still created in the tester's name.
  - **Implementation**: shared form translated English/French; system information gathered in the background with `sysinfo`, each value can be unticked; public excerpt limited to recognised categories (no free text taken from the log), original and unticked fields kept in `private-do-not-upload`. Deliberate difference from Firestorm: explicit review of the data and no automatic sending. Screenshots with/without the interface, images re-encoded without metadata; no screenshot of the login screen.
  - **Tests**: the log filter and the composition of the report (an unticked box = nothing of that information in the text sent) are covered by unit tests.
  - **Human feedback of 2026-10-11**: single-column form without a summary panel, Description / Picture / Data tabs outside the scrolling area, secondary actions grouped and explanations of the sending. Redundant "Edit the description" shortcut removed: the input is edited in the Description tab. Changing "Include the interface" automatically retakes an existing screenshot, keeps its exclusion if any and never replaces an imported file. Labels, displayed technical values and errors follow the language live; the texts entered stay intact. The first test launch forced `--language fr`: removed from the human trials to allow the change in Preferences. Export with English headings kept. Three regression tests added; `check.ps1` green: 839 passed, 12 ignored, Clippy with no warning. `refresh` and `language` scenarios for the comparison screenshots. New human validation expected before the PR.
  - **Draft kept (human feedback of 2026-10-11)**: every new report starts on Description; closing and reopening restores the text, the tab, the scroll position, the checkboxes, the image and the sending mode, including a GitHub authorisation in progress. Kept in memory until the viewer closes or publication is confirmed. A local copy, opening the browser or an error never empties the draft. After creation through the API, reopening prepares a new report; in the browser, "I have published the report" serves as explicit confirmation. The New report shortcut is only offered after confirmed creation. Four regression tests added; `check.ps1` green: 843 tests passed, 12 ignored, Clippy with no warning.
  - **Description without scrolling (human feedback of 2026-10-11)**: global scrolling of the Description tab removed; the four questions stay visible together at the normal size of the form. The three text areas have a fixed height and long descriptions scroll inside their own field. Display test with three descriptions of a hundred lines, including the visibility of the sending controls; `check.ps1` green: 844 tests passed, 12 ignored, Clippy with no warning.
  - **Room for the fields and the image (human feedback of 2026-10-11)**: window enlarged and minimum height raised within the available space; Description fields on three lines with a height of at least 64 points, without scrolling of the form. In Picture, controls on the left and enlarged preview on the right; explanatory note under the screenshot option removed at the human's request. The long descriptions test includes a prepared image to also check the sending controls; `check.ps1` green: 844 tests passed, 12 ignored, Clippy with no warning.
  - **PHP API / Cellar (human choice of 2026-10-11)**: private repository `aurora-api`, Clever Cloud application linked to GitHub and to the domain `api.auroraviewer.com`, private bucket `aurorabugattachments`. Public screenshot and optional private session ZIP, declaration of the hashes in the issue, server-side file checks and verification of GitHub access to the private repository before any ZIP download. No authorisation of the tester passed to the Aurora API. Resumption after a partial failure on the same issue; draft kept until complete success. OAuth configuration done by the human; API deployed, PHP CI green and bucket access check passed. Rust tests over local HTTP: exact files, no GitHub authorisation sent to the storage, resumption without duplicate and preservation of the changes made on GitHub. HTTPS checks: anonymous ZIP refused or redirected to GitHub, sources outside the web root. Screenshots examined: Data and Picture in French, Description in English, all fields and controls visible. Real publication, image in the issue and ZIP download after developer GitHub login confirmed by the human on 2026-10-11.
  - **Repository side**: issue template (`.github/ISSUE_TEMPLATE/`), `bug` and `alpha` labels in `scripts/setup-labels.ps1`, procedure for testers in the README and configuration in `docs/BUG_REPORTING.md`. Demo: `AURORA_DEMO_BUG_REPORT=1|details|image|image-ui|direct` (report generated without sending anything).
  - **Final validation**: `check.ps1` green on 2026-10-11: 848 tests passed, 12 ignored, Clippy with no warning. Tests of the filtering, of the excluded fields, of the public/private folder, of the private ZIP, of links that are too long and of HTTP sending on a local server (text identical to the composed report, authorisation, refusal, limits, lost response). French/English screenshots examined: description, location unticked, scene with/without the interface; CPU cost of the interface about 0.60 ms before opening and 0.66 ms with the form in this demo. `bug` and `alpha` labels created on GitHub. Real direct sending with public image and protected ZIP validated by the human on 2026-10-11; browser mode covered by the automatic tests of link composition and draft preservation.
- ✅ **A single version number, displayed in the window title**: validated by the human in demo on 2026-10-11 (title and Help menu). Release 0.2.0 announced 0.1.0 because the code read `Cargo.toml`.
  - Human choice of 2026-10-10: Git tag as the reference, number of commits and short identifier between two tags (`0.2.0+7.gabcdef123`). Common crate `aurora-version` for the title, the login screen, Help, the logs, the HTTP clients, the media and the comparison of updates; the SL login keeps the four numeric components like `LLVersionInfo::getVersion`, taken from the same source.
  - `--version` prints the number and exits before any window, log or network access. Release: tag supplied at build time and check of the exe's number before publication. Local release build: tags fetched, compiled number written in `version.txt`, same check on a tagged commit.
  - Resolution tested: release, build between tags, pre-release, existing metadata, inconsistent or invalid tag, sources without Git or without a tag; titles and comparison of updates covered. `check.ps1` green on 2026-10-11: 816 tests passed, 12 ignored, Clippy with no warning. Resolution of the build script checked on a temporary Git repository; `--version` and the release verifier checked on the exe (acceptance and refusal). Screenshot of the offline login screen examined: same number in the Version panel and the footer. ARCHITECTURE, README, HUMANS and AGENTS updated. No release published by this task.
- ✅ **Update offered at launch and installed by the viewer** (requested on 2026-10-10, priority): bilingual mockup and real installation 0.2.100 → 0.2.101 validated by the human on 2026-10-11, including required update, blurred background, cache emptied and settings reset. See `docs/UPDATES.md`.
  - **Check and choice**: stable GitHub releases in the background, login screen displayed immediately; pre-releases and `assets-1` ignored. Button disabled and reason visible during the initial check (eight seconds at most) then if an update is required; click, Enter and acceptance of the terms follow the same block. Without a network, the automatic check logs the failure without a window and frees the control. No check for development builds nor the demo. Interface Preferences: ask (Aurora default requested by the human; Firestorm = 0), download automatically then ask for the relaunch, required only. A published minimum version blocks login, even after an old version has been restored.
  - **Finding a skipped version again**: Skip removes only the automatic offer for this tag. Help → Check for updates and the login screen's button offer it again. Later leaves the offer for the next launch; a required update cannot be skipped.
  - **FR / EN interface**: current version in red → new one in green, aligned labels, notes, progress and cancellation. Three choices if the update is optional; only Quit / Update if it is required, with no closing by Escape or outside click. The effects on the cache and the settings are announced separately before installation and relaunch. At the human's request, background blurred in two GPU passes and sharp dialog, without a grey veil. Aurora palette and Phosphor icons; last visual adjustment and installation validated by the human. Demo, dev profile, RTX 3090, 1600×900: median render thread 0.70 → 1.05 ms with the blur, egui encoding 0.05 ms, without the main thread waiting.
  - **Windows installation**: ZIP and SHA-256 published together; hash before bounded extraction, refusal of traversals, links, duplicates and forbidden Windows files. Staging on the same volume as the installation. Helper outside the replaced folder, lock, arguments kept, first presented frame required before deleting the old package; rollback otherwise. Errors and non-portable installation: message and release page.
  - **Cache and settings**: aurora-tools.cmd → Manage releases offers keep / invalidate the cache and keep with migrations / reset the settings. Usual choices: keeping and the viewer's existing migrations. Thresholds written in the annotated tag, carried over to the following releases to cover skipped versions. The helper backs up the data concerned after the parent closes and restores it if startup fails; the other files of the profile are kept.
  - **Firestorm**: `llvvmquery.cpp` and `llvelopack.cpp`, `UpdaterServiceSetting`, `PromptOptionalUpdate`, `OptionalUpdateReady`, `RequiredUpdate`. `llappviewer.cpp`: purge before reading the settings and preservation of the files unrelated to the binary. Aurora uses its existing ZIPs, a Rust helper and an explicit publication policy; Velopack does not match the published package.
  - **Checks**: `check.ps1` green, 850 tests passed and 12 ignored. Demo `AURORA_DEMO_UPDATE=1|progress|ready|ignored`, forced announcements `AURORA_DEMO_UPDATE_MAINTENANCE=cache|settings|both`, HTTP server and throwaway packages, tests of hash, incomplete transfer, cancellation, dangerous archives, lock, restoration of the package and the data, login block and separation of the blurred layers. `scripts/test-update-policy.ps1` covers choices, inheritance, annotated tag and refused threshold, also under Windows PowerShell 5.1. The first human trial validated Skip → Check, Update → Cancel then installation / relaunch; the profile and cache markers were kept and the old package cleaned up. The last required-update trial confirmed cache emptied, default settings, other profile files kept and no transactional backup left. No tag nor release published by this task.

## Repository and tooling

- ✅ `triage` label on incoming issues (project manager's request, 2026-10-27; task `issue-triage-label`, no issue, PR #1189, no effect on the viewer): `.github/workflows/issue-triage.yml` runs `scripts/issue-triage.ps1` on issues opened, reopened, `field_added`, `field_removed` and `unlabeled` (`triage` only). The script reads the issue's Priority (organisation issue field) through the API: an open issue without one carries `triage`, an issue with one does not; issues opened by `Odessa-Draekavik` and `Rohacan` are exempt (`$Exempt`). Manual run for one issue or every open issue, a dry run by default. Label in `scripts/setup-labels.ps1`; rule for the agents in AGENTS.md section 15.
  - ✅ After the merge (2026-10-27): the dry run on `main` read the Priority of the 81 open issues with the workflow's token, as the local run did; then a real run, asked by the human, labelled the 9 open issues without a Priority (#120, #611, #752, #1062, #1063, #1130, #1137, #1157, #1167).
  - ✅ Replaced by the `Triage` Status of the board (task `issue-triage-status`, below): the label was not what the human wanted.
- ✅ AI use policy (human request, 2026-10-28; task `ai-policy`, no issue, PR #1285 and Aurora-Viewer-Website#5, no effect on the viewer): an "AI use policy" page on the website (`/ai-policy/`, English and French, linked from the footer of every page: what AI is used for, what it is never used for, no AI in the release binary, what AGENTS.md is, how a change is tested, how the password is kept) and an "AI use" section in the README that links to it. The page is live since 2026-10-28.
- ✅ **Segmentation fault at every exit on Linux under Wayland** (human request of 2026-10-27, task `wayland-exit-crash`, no issue): `run_app` drops winit's event loop, and with it the Wayland connection, before `main` drops the app; the clipboard of egui-winit (smithay-clipboard) keeps a worker thread on that connection, which then destroyed its objects on a closed connection (`wl_proxy_destroy`, SIGSEGV, core dump; seen on Fedora with a normal exit and with `AURORA_CAPTURE_EXIT`), skipping the cleanup after it (browsers' private profiles). The window, its renderer and egui-winit's state are now dropped in `ApplicationHandler::exiting`, while the event loop still lives. Windows and X11 were not affected. Verified on Fedora (Wayland): three `AURORA_CAPTURE_EXIT` runs and a normal close by the human on 2026-10-27, exit code 0 and no core dump (every exit crashed before).
- ✅ README reorganised (2026-10-13): Download, Discord and Website buttons in the header (same links as the login screen, `links.rs`; SVG files in `docs/readme/`, described in BRANDING.md), a "Why Aurora" section in front for Second Life users (four key figures, then what Aurora does better, without naming another viewer), planned features marked with a "Soon" pill and taken from this file, then Performance, What works today, Roadmap, Using Aurora (the user notes that were scattered under Build), Under the hood and Build. Roadmap brought up to date: group profiles and projector lights are done
- ✅ Feature requests on the feedback board (human request, 2026-10-29; task `fider-feature-requests`, no issue, no effect on the viewer): the GitHub "Feature Request" form is replaced by a link of the new-issue page to https://feedback.aurora-viewer.org (`.github/ISSUE_TEMPLATE/config.yml`); README and AGENTS.md point there. The 85 open Feature issues are copied to the board by a one-off script run by the human (started when assigned, open otherwise), then closed on GitHub with a link.
- ✅ Icon of `aurora-viewer.exe`: the file had no Windows resource (generic icon in Explorer, on a shortcut or a taskbar pin, for GitHub's zip as for `build-release.ps1`); only the window received its icon, at launch. The viewer's `build.rs` now embeds `assets/branding/aurora.ico` and a version block (`winresource`, `rc.exe` from the Windows SDK): name "Aurora Viewer" (Properties, Task Manager) and version of the Git tag, passed by `aurora-version` (`links`), never that of `Cargo.toml`. All profiles are concerned; a failure stops the build, so that a release no longer ships without an icon. Verified on the built exe (1 icon group, version identical to `--version`, including after a new commit); icon and Properties validated by the human in Explorer on 2026-10-13.
- ✅ Automated test windows on Windows: title `Test <task> [AUTOMATION - DO NOT TOUCH]` and dashed yellow outline (`scripts/automation-window-border.ps1`), without taking focus nor intercepting clicks; launch from the worktree documented in `docs/TESTING.md`. Verified under Windows PowerShell 5.1: dashes, position / size tracking, click-through, focus kept, minimise / restore, removal of the tag, hidden window, duplicate and script stopping with the target process.
- ✅ Aurora tools, "Claude permissions": allows in advance the rest of the follow-up required by AGENTS.md, before a refusal of auto mode blocks a session: reading of PRs and CI (`gh pr view / list / checks`, `gh run view / list / watch`), issue triage (`gh issue edit / close / reopen`) and PR screenshots (`pr-captures.ps1`). Ten more `permissions.allow` rules and three more `autoMode.allow` rules; a file that already has the previous rules only receives the new ones. Deliberately left without a rule: `gh api`, `git push`, `gh pr merge`, releases and `end-task.ps1`. Merging of the settings verified on four trial files. AGENTS.md (section 8) and HUMANS.md updated.
- ✅ Aurora tools, "Claude permissions": also allows reading issues (`gh issue list`, `gh issue view`), which Claude Code's auto mode refused to agents ("interference with workloads") as early as the start-of-session follow-up (AGENTS.md, sections 8 and 15). Two more `permissions.allow` rules and one more `autoMode.allow` rule, the latter for a list formatted in PowerShell, which the simple rule does not recognise; a file that already has the previous rules only receives the new ones. Merging of the settings verified on four trial files. AGENTS.md (run these commands alone) and HUMANS.md updated.
- ✅ Aurora tools, "Claude permissions": also allows `gh issue comment`, which Claude Code's auto mode refused to agents ("write to an external system") even though replying to issues is part of their work (AGENTS.md, section 15). One more `permissions.allow` rule and one more `autoMode.allow` rule; a file that already has the PR rules only receives the new ones, the rest of the settings is kept. Merging of the settings verified on four trial files (rules already present, rerun without addition, missing file, unreadable JSON left intact). AGENTS.md and HUMANS.md updated.
- ✅ `aurora-tools.cmd` launcher refreshed after the fixes to the menus and the releases: launch point shared by the scripts of the repository or of the project folder, arguments passed on and exit code kept, including after the standalone installation. Paths containing `!` preserved from `cmd /v:on` and internal variables limited to the launcher. Six isolated cases green, defect reproduced before the fix, tests of the menus / relaunch under PowerShell 5.1 and 7; `check.ps1` green in series (1,068 tests passed, 13 ignored). Three render timing tests failed during the first parallel pass, then passed in series without any change to the engine.
- ✅ Handling of GitHub issues by the agents (AGENTS.md, section 15): reading of the thread and of the screenshots / attached files, analysis, automatic triage by nature / area / state / priority, replies, reasoned closing and reopening; 13 labels added on GitHub, 28 managed by `scripts/setup-labels.ps1` without deleting the others. Real reading of a screenshot and script verified; `check.ps1` green (924 tests passed, 12 ignored).
- ✅ Packaging under Windows PowerShell 5.1: `aurora-install.json` written in UTF-8 without BOM by .NET, without the `utf8NoBOM` option missing from that edition. Destination resolved by PowerShell for relative paths. Defect reproduced before the fix; real packaging verified under Windows PowerShell 5.1 and PowerShell 7 (relative path with spaces after a folder change, schema, version, absence of BOM, hash of the exe and required files). `check.ps1` green: 894 tests passed, 12 ignored. - ✅ Aurora Tools: cursor hidden during navigation, › marker and violet background for the active choice; lines drawn without unintended scrolling, long lists paginated and banner bounded in width. Release in three screens (number, cache, settings), version and previous choices visible, summary before confirmation. After a repair, modified, added or removed scripts trigger the relaunch in a new process of the same PowerShell edition and the same console, GitHub tracking stopped beforehand; even after a later repair error. Tests in hidden Windows consoles from 20×6 to 120×30, ANSI and classic colours, resizing, cancellation, shortcuts, simulated release and isolated relaunch with a path containing spaces: Windows PowerShell 5.1 and PowerShell 7. `test-update-policy.ps1` also checks cancellation between cache and settings; `check.ps1` green, 894 tests passed and 12 ignored. No viewer, tag nor release launched by this task.
- ✅ Public GitHub repository `Aurora-Viewer/Aurora-Viewer` (GPL-3.0-or-later): README, AGENTS.md (rules for the agents), CLAUDE.md, HUMANS.md, ARCHITECTURE.md, docs/BRANDING.md, NOTICE.md; one worktree per task (`scripts/new-task.ps1` / `end-task.ps1`), `scripts/check.ps1` (fmt, clippy -D warnings, tests), `scripts/pr-captures.ps1` (PR screenshots on the `captures` branch), `scripts/fetch-assets.ps1` (emoji font), type + area labels (`scripts/setup-labels.ps1`); GitHub Actions CI (Windows: fmt, clippy, tests with a `ci` profile that is fast to build, skipped for documentation PRs; gitleaks), release on SemVer tag (Windows zip, notes by type), weekly Dependabot
- ✅ Workspace: 2024 edition, common lints, stable Rust (`rust-toolchain.toml`), rustfmt (140 columns), clippy at zero warnings
- ✅ Disk space: debug info reduced to line numbers (`line-tables-only`, debug and release), builds only in the task's worktree (AGENTS.md rule), `scripts/clean.ps1` (shows the space taken by the target folders, frees it with `-Apply`, only touches folders marked CACHEDIR.TAG, refuses during a build); 78 GB freed on 2026-10-08
- ✅ A single local build profile: `check.ps1`, the tests and the demo go through the `dev` profile (without `--release`), set up like the published version (without debug assertions nor overflow checks, without a console); no more double `target\debug` + `target\release` per worktree. `new-task.ps1` frees the `target` of tasks merged or inactive for 7 days. `scripts/build-release.ps1` (and `RELEASE\build-release.bat`) builds the latest `main` from GitHub, or a PR, in `--release` in `..\RELEASE\`; `scripts/package.ps1` shared with the GitHub release
- ✅ Repository in the `Aurora-Viewer` organisation (`Devs` team, maintain right); fully automatic merge: the agent merges its PR itself (`scripts/merge-pr.ps1`, verdict ✅, human validation except for a PR with no effect on the viewer) through the merge queue of `main`, protected without exception; two-tier CI (PR: fmt + clippy + secrets; merge queue and `main`: + tests; jobs in parallel, cache written from `main` only); gitleaks on the command line (the action requires a licence for an organisation); releases on request by the agent. Measured on 2026-10-09: CI of a PR 2 min 39 s (against 8 to 9 min), merge queue 5 min 43 s, PR #15 merged without a single click
- ✅ README refocused on presenting the viewer: sections "How Aurora differs from Firestorm" (engine, performance measured on 2026-10-10, interface, features specific to Aurora), "What works today" and "Roadmap" (not there yet, planned). The `AURORA_*` test options and the profiling, whose table was unreadable on GitHub (cells of a paragraph each), are in `docs/TESTING.md`: a short table per topic, the details below, all in French; the "Demo" choice of the Aurora tools reads this file (same scenarios as before)
- ✅ One-line installation (`scripts/setup.ps1`, command in HUMANS.md and the README): table of what is found and what will be done, in Aurora's colours; current folder offered, otherwise path asked for; installs what is missing with winget (Git and GitHub CLI for the machine, Visual Studio C++ tools, Rust through rustup), clones the repository and Firestorm side by side, emoji font, toolchain and crates, GitHub login and noreply git identity; can be rerun (updates without reinstalling). Docs reviewed: no more hard-coded path, review "PR CI", PR template and Dependabot aligned with the merge by the agent
- ✅ Aurora tools, GitHub: live PR tracking (one GraphQL query every 10 to 20 s in the background, without freezing the screen; panel adapted to the window width with CI and duration, merge queue, review verdict, latest merges; summary in the bottom banner; Windows notifications: PR merged, CI red, left the queue, new PR, main red; "Aurora PR" shortcut); release management (write permission verified, green main required, SemVer number proposed from the labels of the PRs, tracking of the Release workflow; deletion with the tag typed, `assets-1` protected; `-DryRun`)
- ✅ Aurora tools, repository reset: inventory of what would be lost (uncommitted changes, commits never pushed per branch, stashes, tasks with changes or commits, ignored files outside builds), refusal if a build or a viewer is running, word RESET to type; tasks and RELEASE\ deleted, repository put back on origin/main (reset --hard, clean -ffdx, branches and stashes deleted; the folder is kept: the tools run from it), then repair; git identity, GitHub login and Firestorm kept; `-DryRun`
- ✅ `--title <text>`: window title and separate log (`aurora-demo-<text>.log`) for the agents' test windows
- ✅ Demo mode has its own settings and its own cache (`config\demo`, `cache\demo`): the tests no longer touch the user's; names of the demo avatars are fictitious
- ✅ `symphonia` 0.5 → 0.6: new API (AudioDecoder, probe, generic buffer copied as interleaved f32, StandardTag); `id3v2` feature enabled (0.5 always read the ID3v2 at the head of MP3s); output clipped to ±1 like the 16-bit PCM of ov_read (LLVorbisDecodeState::decodeSection), as 0.6 no longer does it. Verified on 85 Ogg Vorbis sounds from the cache: samples identical to 0.5, except the encoder's end padding now removed (0 to 46 ms, exact length like libvorbis). symphonia's log limited to warnings (two INFO lines per sound in 0.6). Listened to on Agni on 2026-10-09: object sounds, collisions, radio stream, good quality, no sound cut off
- ✅ `webrtc` 0.17 → 0.21 (rewrite around the I/O-free crate `rtc`): PeerConnectionBuilder, callbacks in a PeerConnectionEventHandler, data channel and received track read in a loop (poll), SSRC and payload type of the local track supplied by us; llwebrtc's UDP range 60000-60100 kept (a free port drawn at random, as the EphemeralUDP allocator no longer exists), IPv6 only with a routable address, new attempt without mDNS if its socket does not open, microphone sent from the first Connected state. SDP offer identical in substance. Tested on Agni on 2026-10-09: sending and receiving, reconnection to the voice of the new region on a sim change

## Network and protocol

- ✅ Links written with their own text, `[https://example.org Text]` and `[secondlife:///app/... Text]` (#417; LLUrlEntryHTTPLabel, LLUrlEntrySLLabel, indra/llui/llurlentry.cpp): profiles (About, Favorites, Real life, both window styles), conversations, chat bubbles and the search details show the text, which opens the link inside (web link with its trust badge and the address in its tooltip, avatar link, place link with the text as its label); the text is unescaped (`%20`), and a text that is itself a link shows the real address instead, as in the Legacy Viewer. Addresses without a scheme, "www.example.org", are links too (LLUrlEntryHTTPNoProtocol, opened as http://). A closing bracket no longer ends a bare link. Demo: `AURORA_DEMO_PROFILE=friend` (About). Validated by the human in demo on 2026-10-16
- ✅ Teleport within the same region: direct move without screen or sound, like LLAgent::teleportCore(is_local); requests by name, maps, history and chat commands, TeleportLocal and TeleportFinish events in the current region. Deliberate difference for unresolved destinations (landmark, home, invitation, script): screen deferred until another region is confirmed by TeleportFinish, so as never to hide a local teleport. UISndTeleportOut sound removed for local teleports at the human's request (deliberate difference from Firestorm), kept once only on region changes; unknown destination: sound deferred until TeleportFinish. Demo (after the initial loading): AURORA_DEMO_TP=1 or x,y,z arrives immediately; remote keeps the inter-region progress. Checks: check.ps1 green (5 new tests), screenshots of local without screen and inter-region with progress; UISndTeleportOut traces: 0 locally, 1 between regions; validated by the human on 2026-10-09 in "Test teleportation-locale-directe".
- ✅ XML-RPC login, reliable UDP circuit (acks, resends, pings), multi-simulator — repeated logins on Agni
- ✅ Capabilities (seed, 25 on Young Island), EventQueueGet, teleport
- ✅ Object updates (full, compressed, terse, cached), terrain, names
- ✅ EEP environment per region (the region's cycle loaded on Agni)
- ✅ EEP **per parcel** like LLEnvironment (`world/eep_env.rs`, recordEnvironment): the region and the parcel each have their environment (parcel > region); a parcel without a cycle of its own (or with an invalid cycle: water track or ground sky track empty) no longer erases the region's (at Yikes, Aurora showed the night of the default sky instead of dusk); region without a valid cycle: Firestorm's default cycle (asset `5646d39e…`, loaded through ViewerAsset `settings_id`, in the disk cache, 4 h, offset 16 h), pending built-in default settings; the simulator's sun is now only used for regions without EEP (Firestorm ignores it); stale responses ignored (other region, parcel left); the region's ExtEnvironment requested when the capabilities arrive and at each RegionInfo (requestRegion), the parcel's on a change of parcel / of version; fades like DayTransition: 5 s between environments, 1 s during a teleport, 5 s between altitude tracks (the region's altitudes, also for the parcel); keys: position clamped to 0..1 (a key at 1.0 stays at the end of the day), the last key of a same position wins; sky without `sun_rotation` kept (default rotation); `track_altitudes` missing = 0; local time presets = local environment (with fade). Demo: `AURORA_DEMO_EEP_PARCEL=1|parcel|default|stale`. To verify on Agni: Yikes at dusk next to Firestorm, parcel with its own environment, teleports, altitude tracks — validated on Agni on 2026-10-09 (Yikes, same time of day as Firestorm; empty response from the parcel without effect; presets with fade)
- ✅ Avatar on Agni (Yikes, 2026-10-09): white jacket and skirt in Aurora, blue denim in Firestorm. The textures of their texture entries do not exist on the CDN (403; probably leftovers of the creator's local textures): Firestorm never requests them for a glTF face. Difference fixed (LLViewerObject::updateTEMaterialTextures / initRenderMaterial, LLGLTFMaterialList::getMaterial, LLVOVolume): a face with a glTF material never again displays the diffuse of its texture entry (nor its legacy normal / specular); while the asset is loading, default material (white, override pending like Firestorm); asset not found (403 / 404, abandoned at once like LL's asset storage), invalid or failing repeatedly: default material + override, so the texture of the override (PRIM_GLTF_BASE_COLOR on a blank material) is displayed all the same. Diagnostics: abandonment of a material logged (HTTP status only), counter "glTF materials ready / pending / missing" in the streaming summary, and for each glTF face of the attachments the material, its state and the override ("glTF face" lines). Tests: state machine of the loading, rendered material (pending / not found / loaded + override), RENDER_MATERIAL block, texture entry fields with a bitfield over several bytes. Validated on Agni on 2026-10-09 (jacket and skirt in blue denim like Firestorm). ⬜ Later: IMG_DEFAULT `d2114404…`, a local texture of the viewer that Firestorm does not download and that Aurora requests from the CDN)
- ✅ Asset downloads aligned with Firestorm (llcorehttp / LLTextureFetch): separate client without automatic decompression (Firestorm turns off CURLOPT_ENCODING), redirects followed while keeping the range, 416 = end of data, 499–599 and network errors retried 5 times (1 to 16 s, Retry-After < 30 s), range resumed 1 byte before the end of the data like LLTextureFetchWorker; each failed texture logged once with its HTTP code and the host (never the URL). Validated by the human on Agni on 2026-10-09 (textures, avatars, teleports normal; log of the 403s)
- ✅ Environment picker like Firestorm (FloaterQuickPrefs of quickprefs.cpp, LLEnvironment::setEnvironment / setSharedEnvironment / saveToSettings, LLFloaterEnvironmentAdjust; `world/env_select.rs`, `world/eep_env.rs`, `ui/environment.rs`): "Environment" window (World › Environment › Environment picker…) with the lists "Sky environment / Water environment / Day/Night", arrows ‹ › (stepComboBox: the two fixed entries are skipped, it wraps around), return to the shared environment, "Personal lighting…" and the time presets. Lists: every settings item (asset 56, type sky 0 / water 1 / day cycle 2 read from the flags) of the Library and the inventory, outside the Trash and the Marketplace listings, only one per asset, sorted like Firestorm's std::multimap (byte by byte: upper case before lower case); "Shared environment" and "Based on day cycle" / "Nothing" not selectable, displayed like setSelectedEnvironment. On first opening, the folders are loaded (FetchInventoryDescendents2 / FetchLibDescendents2, 16 per request, 48 in flight at most): the Library's "Environments" first, then the Settings folder, then the rest of the inventory. Applied as a local environment (assets through ViewerAsset `settings_id`, disk cache) according to the asset type: a day cycle replaces everything, a sky or a water is fixed on top (the missing half frozen on what is displayed); choices applied in order (a sky chosen after a cycle stays on top), a new choice in the same list replaces the one still loading, asset not found = FailedToFindSettings message, nothing changes. Fade: FSEnvironmentManualTransitionTime (Preferences › Graphics › "Environment fade", 0 s = immediate by default like Firestorm, also for the time presets, which used to fade in 5 s), return to the shared environment in 5 s; the local environment stays across region changes and teleports, and from one session to the next (EnvironmentPersistAcrossLogin, "Keep environment", enabled; the account's local_environment_data.bin file: asset ids, or sky / water settings without an asset). "Personal lighting": freezes the displayed sky and water (local cycle included), ambient, sun, blue horizon / density, clouds (colours at Firestorm's scale), haze, cloud coverage and size, probe ambiance, brightness / HDR scale, azimuth / elevation of the sun and the moon, scale, glow, stars; "Reset" confirmed = shared environment. Deliberate differences: a modified sky is displayed as "Personal lighting" (Firestorm keeps the item's name); an item without an asset is not listed; changes to an already active local environment also follow the chosen fade. Demo: `AURORA_DEMO_ENV_SELECT=1|lighting|list`. To verify on Agni: several skies / waters / cycles from the Library and the inventory, arrows, shared environment, teleport and reconnection (environment kept) — validated on Agni on 2026-10-09 (lists, application, return to the shared environment, personal lighting; instant presets and sorting like Firestorm confirmed)
- ✅ Parcel properties (ParcelProperties) → "About Land", parcel music and voice channel
- ✅ Per-region object cache (like LLVOCache, CRC) + "cache empty" flag at the handshake (3460 objects re-read for Young Island)
- ✅ Touch (ObjectGrab/DeGrab), sit (AgentRequestSit / AvatarSitResponse) — validated by the human online on 2026-10-09
- ✅ SimulatorFeatures (voice server type) and voice capabilities (ProvisionVoiceAccountRequest, VoiceSignalingRequest): WebRTC session negotiated on Agni
- ✅ TeleportProgress and TeleportFinish events passed up to the viewer (teleport screen with progress on Agni)
- ✅ Outfit restored at login (like updateAppearanceFromCOF): "Current outfit" folder re-read, missing linked objects worn (RezMultipleAttachmentsFromInv, one per message, point = last worn, retry after 30 s), bake requested from the server (UpdateAvatarAppearance with the COF version, retry if the server expects another one), dummy AgentIsNowWearing on SL; nothing at the first login — on Agni: COF version 383, 18 links, 9 objects worn, complete avatar

## Rendering

- ✅ HUD textures at full resolution (tester report, 2026-10-29; task `hud-texture-resolution`; [PR #1403](https://github.com/Aurora-Viewer/Aurora-Viewer/pull/1403); validated by the human on the grid on 2026-10-29): a HUD's textures were loaded for the size of their prim on screen, so its small buttons (often one corner of a shared atlas) stayed three levels too coarse (`want 3` in the J2 report). As the Legacy Viewer (`LLVOVolume::updateTextureVirtualSize`, BOOST_HUD: 2048², discard 0), every texture of a worn HUD now asks for its full resolution, never lowered by the memory budget. Unit test `hud_textures_load_at_full_resolution_even_under_memory_pressure`.
- ✅ Sky shaking while walking or turning ([#1059](https://github.com/Aurora-Viewer/Aurora-Viewer/issues/1059), 2026-10-26; task `sky-stutter`; validated by the human on the grid on a skybox near 2 000 m on 2026-10-26). The sky shader took each pixel's direction from the full inverse view-projection minus the camera position, both in f32 region coordinates: on a skybox 2 447 m up (the report) the direction moved by up to ~1.2 pixel from one frame to the next as the camera moved (0.3 pixel near the ground), so clouds, rainbow, sun and stars shook. The frame now carries `inv_view_proj_rot`, the inverse of the projection by the view's rotation alone (computed in f64), and the sky reads camera-relative directions from it (< 0.02 pixel; unit test `sky_directions_hold_still_high_above_the_ground`). No change to the sky's look.
- ✅ Flipbook textures loaded for the size of one frame (#1229; 2026-10-28; task `animated-texture-sharpness`): a texture animation with a frame grid (llSetTextureAnim without ROTATE or SCALE) shows one frame of its texture at a time, but its face was sized with the texture entry's repeats, as if the whole texture covered it: a 2048 px 4 × 4 flipbook (512 px frames) was loaded four times too coarse each way and blurred a few metres away. The face's repeats for texture streaming are now those of the grid, the smaller of 1 / size_x and 1 / size_y (`TexAnimParams::frame_repeats`, `RecordAnim::frame_repeats`), within the usual bounds (`texture_importance::per_repeats`). Visible difference from the Legacy Viewer, on purpose: it does so only at rate 0, where LLVOVolume::animateTextures writes the frame into the texture entry's scale; a running animation keeps the entry's scale in LLViewerTextureList::updateImageDecodePriority and blurs the same way. ROTATE, SCALE and grid-less scrolling keep the texture entry's repeats. The demo textures are 64 px (no discard level to show): unit test, then validated by the human on Agni on 2026-10-28 (a flipbook seen from a distance stays as crisp as a still texture)
- ✅ Invisible mesh transparency in animesh linksets (human report and clarification, 2026-10-17; task `animesh-transparency`): restore zero-alpha face records when Show transparency is enabled and release them when disabled, following LLViewHighlightTransparent / LLPipeline::rebuildDrawInfo. Non-rigged mesh children of animated linksets appear with the main overlay alone; retain the separate rigged-overlay option for skinned surfaces. Add `AURORA_DEMO_ANIMESH=alpha|alpha-cycle` for invisible non-rigged mesh children on ground/worn animesh, an invisible skinned neighbour and activation/cleanup/filter/reactivation after loading. Full `check.ps1` green: 3,357 passed, zero failures, 26 ignored; scripts, formatting and Clippy clean. Default dev build succeeds. Inspected four-state CPU/GPU captures: both unrigged meshes appear with the main overlay, disappear when disabled and return when enabled; skinned faces follow the separate filter. Explicit diagnostic run exits successfully with no warnings/errors: each unrigged child has six red face records with either rigged setting and zero records while the overlay is off. Validated by the human on the reported animesh on 2026-10-17; merge explicitly authorized.
- ✅ Wind on particles (#663; task `particle-wind`): sample the containing region's `WindGrid` at each live `LL_PART_WIND_MASK` particle through `ParticleSource::update_with_wind`, retaining `LLViewerPartGroup::updateParticles` velocity relaxation and `LLWind::getVelocity` interpolation/scaling. Neighboring grids, updated wind and particles outliving their source share the path; outside known regions use the main region, as in the Legacy Viewer. Unflagged particles keep their motion. Regression fails with the original zero-wind scene path and passes with the fix. Full `scripts/check.ps1` green: 4,298 passed, zero failures, 26 ignored; scripts, formatting and Clippy clean. Native `AURORA_DEMO_PARTICLES=region-wind|region-calm` and reversed `AURORA_DEMO_WIND=0,3` captures inspected: only the flagged plume drifts/reverses, ordinary smoke and the fountain remain intact; all runs exit 0 without warnings/errors, 60 FPS retained at the cap, particle simulation about 0.02 ms/frame. The comparison frames both emitters automatically. Validated by the human in the online test viewer on 2026-10-22; merge authorized.
- ✅ Avatar render settings (issue #664; PR #1097; task `avatar-render-settings-664`): World > Avatar render settings lists the saved always / never full-detail exceptions, including distant residents, with asynchronous names, name/UUID filtering, sortable virtualized rows, Ctrl/Shift selection, a shared resident picker and batch edits/removal. Follow `FSFloaterAvatarRenderSettings` and `FSAvatarRenderPersistenceMenu::changeRenderSetting`; share the existing settings and complexity rules with avatar menus, save a batch once, ignore own/nil residents and honor RLVa name privacy. English/French labels; demo `AURORA_DEMO_AVATAR_RENDER_SETTINGS=1|empty`. Full `scripts/check.ps1` green: 5,566 passed, zero failures, 30 ignored; scripts, formatting and Clippy clean. Five regressions cover settings persistence/render-rule use, selection and external removal at two UI scales, filtering/sorting/privacy, picker ownership and adding multiple distant friends. Default dev executable built; native FR/EN populated, empty-list and neighbouring Sound Explorer captures inspected, all exit 0. Validated by the maintainer in the test viewer on 2026-10-26; merge explicitly authorized.
- ✅ Mouse wheel in HUD editing: without the HUD key held, zoom out / zoom back in of the view common to all HUDs, including from an empty area; local zoom takes priority with the HUD key. Local profiles and offsets kept, LLAgentCamera behaviour followed (factor per notch, 10–100% bounds of llviewerdisplay). Demo `AURORA_DEMO_HUDS=edit-scroll`: off-screen HUD at 200%, view zoomed out to 32.8%, local zoom at 220% without changing the view, global return to 100% with the local factor kept; no interception outside editing or with a selection of world objects. `check.ps1` green: 905 tests passed, 12 ignored, formatting / Clippy green; screenshots before / zoom out / local zoom / zoom back in and neighbouring demos `edit-zoom|drag-zoom|scale-copy` examined, camera unchanged, no GPU error. Human validation online received on 2026-10-11 before the PR.
- ✅ Per-HUD local zoom requested on 2026-10-11: configurable move key + mouse wheel, range 10–800%, fine steps with Shift, frame and percentage on hover, return to 100% and forgetting of the profile. Visual transformation of the whole linkset and of its text, usable without Modify / Move rights, without changing the server dimensions; local profiles per account, inventory name and creator, reapplied to new copies, worn copies independent. Picking / touch and build tools follow the visual while keeping the server coordinates; planar textures unchanged. Extension specific to Aurora, distinct from the global HUD zoom of LLAgentCamera. `check.ps1` green: 904 tests passed, 12 ignored, formatting / Clippy green; screenshots before / zoom / new copy / touch / return to 100% examined, camera and scripts unchanged during the wheel, no GPU error. Demo `AURORA_DEMO_HUDS=scale|scale-copy|scale-touch|scale-reset|scale-forget|scale-ctrl|scale-key|scale-secondary`. Human validation online received on 2026-10-11 before the PR.
- ✅ Preferences › Convenience: HUD move key configurable from the keyboard (ALT by default, Ctrl / Shift and ordinary keys accepted), choice saved, Restore ALT button and Esc to cancel the key capture. Hover and the start of the gesture use the same setting; releasing the key during a move keeps the hold until the left button is released. Compatibility of old settings and keeping of the choice covered by the tests. check.ps1 green: 785 tests passed, 12 ignored, formatting / Clippy green; screenshot of the Convenience section and ALT, drag-ctrl and drag-key demos examined, same position saved, with no script / camera nor GPU error. Trials at 60 FPS, with no optimisation test. Online tests validated by the human on 2026-10-10, before the PR.
- ✅ Direct move of HUDs: ALT + left click held on a visible element, Phosphor grab hand on hover, movement of the root and of its whole linkset in the screen plane; saved on release by the same position MultipleObjectUpdate as LLManipTranslate, without triggering the scripts / media nor the camera. The Move right is enough for a HUD without the Modify right. The gesture continues after ALT is released and ends on loss of focus; detaching / replacing invalidates the target. Shortcut specific to Aurora requested by the human; Firestorm coordinates, permissions and protocol kept. Demo AURORA_DEMO_HUDS=drag|drag-zoom|drag-ignore. PR #92 integrated: rendering of back faces and double-sided exceptions kept; ALT picking covered for back faces, double-sided materials / overrides, IGNORE and hidden HUDs. Local conversion shared with the gizmos via edit_parent. `check.ps1` green (783 tests passed, 12 ignored), tests of the linkset, coordinates / zoom / resizing / rotation of the attachment point, rights, server echo, invalidated target and network format; screenshots before / during / after examined; after the integration of PR #92, ALT move at normal size / half zoom, front / back / double-sided faces after a half turn and gizmo at half zoom re-verified, with no GPU error. Touch and hiding verified previously. All the trials of this task are capped at 60 fps (`--fps-limit 60`), with no measurement of rendering optimisation. Online tests validated by the human on 2026-10-10, before the PR; direct move and Convenience preferences accepted.
- ✅ HUDs, editing by right-click › Edit: selection of the linkset, handles and outlines in the screen projection, move relative to the attachment point and MultipleObjectUpdate sent on release; local values in the Object tab. Zoom, resizing, linked parts and HUD without the modify right (moving the linkset allowed, individual modification protected) taken into account; HUD selection separate from the world. Sources: LLManipTranslate, LLSelectMgr and LLViewerJointAttachment. Demo `AURORA_DEMO_HUDS=menu|edit|edit-zoom`. Local trials limited to 60 FPS (`AURORA_FPS_LIMIT=60`), with no profiling nor rendering optimisation test. check.ps1 green (777 tests passed, 12 ignored); screenshots before / menu / editing / move and half zoom examined, HUD touch and linked touch of the world re-verified, no GPU validation error. Move and back-face fix validated by the human on Agni on 2026-10-10, agreement for the PR and the merge; persistence after reattachment to verify in the additional tests below.
- ✅ HUDs, back faces: turned-over panels displayed mirrored, fixed after the human's report on Agni on 2026-10-10. Back faces culled in rendering and in picking, exception for glTF materials / overrides that are explicitly double-sided, like LLGLSPipeline / LLRenderPass::pushGLTFBatch. check.ps1 green (777 tests passed, 12 ignored); demos `AURORA_DEMO_HUDS=faces|faces-turned`, opaque, translucent and masked, screenshots before / after the half turn, move and touch examined, no GPU validation error. Local trials at 60 FPS, with no profiling nor optimisation; validated by the human on their real HUD on Agni on 2026-10-10 before the PR.
- ✅ Legacy materials (RenderMaterials capability, like LLMaterialMgr): creator's alpha mode (none / blend / mask + cutoff / emissive mask), normal and specular maps with their own repeats / offsets / rotations, shininess and environment (approximated in PBR). Works on Agni (details of the trousers). Rate like LLMaterialMgr (fixes the burst of 503s on arrival): one GET of all the materials of the region first, then the missing ones in batches (MaxMaterialsPerTransaction, 50 by default), one request per region every 1/RenderMaterialsCapability s (1 per second by default, read from SimulatorFeatures), 2 requests in flight in total. Difference: after an HTTP error the region is paused for 10 s and the materials requested again after 30 s (Firestorm waits 5 min, and 20 min if the GET of the region fails). Verified on Agni on 2026-10-08: no more 503s, 3 regions loaded in one GET each (107, 1378 and 82 materials), nothing left pending
- ✅ wgpu on Vulkan, bindless textures, sub-allocated geometry, multi-draw-indirect
- ✅ PBR (glTF: opaque / mask + cutoff / blend, double-sided), legacy alpha (transparency of the face, alpha of the texture detected: opaque / mask / blend), prims, sculpties, mesh with LOD (LLVOVolume::calcLOD logic), rigged mesh, animesh, Bento
- ✅ Avatars: skeleton, animations, server bakes
- ✅ Animesh: ObjectAnimation capability announced at the seed like LLViewerRegionImpl::buildCapabilityNames to receive the animations from the simulator. Standalone skeleton and controller for each root, even when worn; offsets of the root mesh included and animesh excluded from the wearer's offsets / BOM masks. Animations of the prims of the linkset merged (maximum sequence, continuous phase and stop / resume kept), procedural motions disabled like LLControlAvatar. Shadows of alpha rigged faces with cutoff / dithering and the same texture coordinates as the visible pass (planar / PBR mapping included), bounds computed on the pose and exclusion from the static probes; bindings and offsets recomputed after a change of parent / flag or the arrival of the skin. Reference: LLVOVolume::isAnimatedObject, LLControlAvatar::updateAnimations / matchVolumeTransform, LLVOAvatar::updateAttachmentOverrides and LLDrawPoolAvatar::renderShadow. Demo AURORA_DEMO_ANIMESH=1 (root, linked mesh animated by a child, worn animesh), screenshots before / after and standard / loop demos verified with no GPU error; 7 tests added, check.ps1 green (331 passed, 4 ignored after the integration of main), test of the LLSD POST on a local server failing before / passing after. Validated on the grid by the human on 2026-10-09: animations and shadows on the ground, worn animations, avatar intact after attaching / wearing / detaching.
- ✅ Animation loops: sequence clock and continuous playback clock separated (same active UUID = phase and ease-in kept; observed stop = restart), end of the automatic restarts of finished non-looped animations; phase and modulo in double precision, with no artificial minimum duration; stop from the current phase, residual weight kept during the ease-in, duplicated quantised keys = last value. Reference: LLVOAvatar::processAnimationStateChanges, LLMotionController::startMotion / updateMotionsByType, LLKeyframeMotion::onUpdate / deserialize. Explicit adaptation: periodic interpolation of only the key-less intervals of each curve, with intro / outro values at the bounds of partial loops; explicit keys and discontinuities kept. 17 tests added (poses and palettes within ±1 µs over 100 cycles, partial / very short loops after several hours, sequences / stops between frames, loading, fades and blend budgets); check.ps1 green (279 tests passed, 4 ignored). Demo AURORA_DEMO_ANIM_LOOP=1: sequences every 120 frames, stop / restart at frames 960 / 1020; screenshots of the scenario and of the standard demo examined, GPU validation with no error. Fix validated by the human on 2026-10-09 in "Test boucles-animations", after a request to test prolonged running, walk / stop / resume and AO.
- ✅ Animation blending like LLMotionController / LLJointStateBlender: ease-in and ease-out fades (cubic_step, residual weight), a stopped animation continues in its ease-out, an animation not yet loaded starts when it arrives, non-looped animations stop on their own before the end, priorities per bone (6 states at most, the most recent first at equal priority), and a bone without animation keeps its last pose instead of going back to the T-pose (cause of the T-pose when starting / stopping walking, especially with an AO)
- ✅ Skinning like SL (LLSkinningUtil): bind matrices specific to each mesh × matrices of the avatar's bones (before: bind assumed equal to the default skeleton → hands / fingers stretched on the grid) — fixed and verified on Agni
- ✅ Bone offsets imposed by worn meshes (alt_inverse_bind_matrix, like addAttachmentOverridesForObject), recomputed every 2 s; pelvis ignored
- ✅ Shape (avatar_lad.xml, like LLVisualParam / LLDriverParam / LLPolySkeletalDistortion): 253 parameters transmitted, driver parameters, gender, bones scaled and moved (inherited by the collision volumes), "volume morphs" (belly, butt, hips, chest… for fitted bodies), pelvis height according to the body size and the Hover (computeBodySize) — verified on Agni with Reborn, Legacy, LeLutka Evox head
- ✅ Base SL body hidden when a worn object displays its bake (LLVOAvatar::updateMeshVisibility)
- ✅ Culling of rigged meshes on the extent of the wearing avatar (the bind space can be anywhere: Evox head at 170 m) — no more parts disappearing when zooming on Agni
- ✅ Standard transparent texture (8dcd4a48…) invisible: unused layers of mesh heads and bodies
- ✅ Bump / shininess / PBR maps at the same resolution as the colour texture
- ✅ Lighting of "classic" skies like Firestorm (calcAtmosphericVars, classic softenLightF: sky ambient, sun da^1.2, atmospheric haze hazeF, no tonemap when the probe ambiance is 0), "linear" mode; terrain included — compared with Firestorm on Agni (remaining difference: side faces, further down)
- ✅ Bump byte of the faces fixed: fullbright = bit 5, shiny = bits 6-7 (before: swapped → chrome hand)
- ✅ Mesh: PARAMS_MESH parameter (0x60) sent by the servers read as a sculpty (before: all meshes drawn as their base prim) — game table and chairs of the arcade correct on Agni
- ✅ Downloads: mesh, materials and animations go before textures; texture at full resolution = whole file (like LLTextureFetch), no more full-resolution decoding from a partial file (blurry textures fixed on Agni)
- ✅ Non-rigged attachments that follow the animated bone of their point (hair, hats, held objects), poses computed before the transforms: no one-frame lag
- ✅ Automatic texture memory according to the detected VRAM (Vulkan): 70% while leaving at least 1.5 GB for the rest (integrated: 2 GB, unknown: 1.5 GB), manual setting possible
- ✅ Outlines of rezzed objects that jitter by about one pixel, several times per second, with the Alt camera fixed on the object (reported by the human on 2026-10-12 at the BackBone Main Store, at 2,106 m; nearby objects only, not the large distant objects; with SMAA, MSAA 4x — less — and without anti-aliasing; already in the version of 2026-10-10). Two causes. (1) Camera at rest: the smoothing used glam's `Vec3::lerp` (`a * (1 - k) + b * k`), which does not return `a` when both ends are equal; depending on the frame time, the camera moved back then returned by one step of its coordinates (0.008 to 0.015 mm) in about one frame out of four, for four positions out of ten. Measured on the grid with `AURORA_STILL_CHECK=1`: camera moved in 14 to 36 frames per second, target and avatar motionless. Fixed with `lerp_monotonic` (camera smoothing and transitions, pull-back in flight, damping of our avatar); test `a_camera_at_rest_stays_bit_identical_whatever_the_frame_time`. Validated by the human on the grid on 2026-10-12: no more jitter at rest. (2) Camera in motion: the vertices were computed in region coordinates, where single precision only holds 0.24 mm at that altitude; each vertex of the nearby objects landed up to one pixel off, somewhere else at each change of view (this is also what made the step of cause 1 visible). The object vertex shader now removes the camera position from the model translation (`eye_model`), and the main camera's view is reduced to its rotation (`eye_view_proj`): error below 0.01 px instead of 0.2 to 1.4 px (test `eye_relative_vertices_land_on_their_pixel_high_above_the_ground`). The records, the culling, the lighting and the shadows stay in region coordinates; better than Firestorm, which keeps this step per object. In demo at 2,100 m, on a very slow close-up orbit, the edge of a panel goes backwards 30 times over 100 frames instead of 277. Demo: `AURORA_DEMO_ALTITUDE`, `AURORA_DEMO_CAMERA=close|drift,x,y`, `AURORA_DEMO_AGENT_JITTER`; comparison: `AURORA_EYE_RELATIVE=0`. Validated by the human on the grid on 2026-10-12, at the same place: no more jitter at rest, nor during camera moves and zooms
- ✅ Patches of sky on the terrain (reported by the human on 2026-10-12 at KB, on the sand around 106 m: large sheets of a flat blue, without shadows, with jagged edges). Regression from the previous line, with any anti-aliasing without MSAA (none, FXAA, TAA, SMAA; the default setting, MSAA 4x, was not affected, hence correct demo screenshots). The depth prepass draws the terrain with `vs_depth`, which had moved to the camera-relative computation, whereas the scene pass still drew it in region coordinates (`vs_terrain`); without MSAA, that pass tests the terrain against the depth of the prepass, and the fragments whose depth differed by a rounding were rejected: the sky showed through the ground, at any altitude (more so higher up). `vs_terrain` now computes its position like `vs_depth`, operation for operation (test `terrain_position_is_the_depth_prepass_position`); in passing, the terrain gains the objects' precision at altitude. Demo: `AURORA_AA=6` with `AURORA_DEMO_POS="60,128"` (and `AURORA_DEMO_ALTITUDE=86`). Validated by the human on the grid on 2026-10-12, at the same place, with SMAA: sand intact
- ✅ Cascaded shadows (adjustable quality), MSAA/FXAA/TAA, Khronos PBR Neutral / ACES
- ✅ Depth prepass, SSAO, SSR (option)
- ✅ Water: transparency, refraction, SL fog, waves (EEP normal map), planar reflections
- ✅ Mirrors (SL mirror probes), adjustable resolution
- ✅ Full EEP sky (SL atmosphere, clouds, sun/moon, stars), altitude tracks, day/night
- ✅ Particles (port of LLPartSysData / LLViewerPartSim)
- ✅ Wind particles missing around the motorbike (2026-10-09): extended format of compressed updates decoded after the textures and their animation like LLVOVolume::processUpdateMessage (before: source ignored, including with a glow of 0.01); FOLLOW_VELOCITY oriented in the view plane like LLVOPartGroup::getGeometry, without shortening the streaks when their velocity points towards the camera. Demo: AURORA_DEMO_PARTICLES=wind|smoke|both|legacy (script parameters, local soft texture). Decoding, truncated-data and simulation tests; 510 tests passed, 7 ignored, formatting and clippy green; before / after screenshots and GPU validation with no error. Fix tested in-game and validated by the human on 2026-10-09 after a requested check of the wind around the motorbike and of the smoke, from the side and in the direction of travel.
- ✅ Point lights, anisotropic filtering, draw distance, maximum number of avatars
- ✅ Hover text (llSetText) like LLHUDText
- ✅ Glow like Firestorm (LLDrawPoolGlow + generateGlow/combineGlow): face glow accumulated in the scene's alpha, extracted after gamma, 4-pass blur on 512×512 8-bit, strength 0.325; invisible faces with glow shine too; "Glow" option — compared with Firestorm on Agni (identical RenderGlow* settings)
- ✅ Glow: glow pass without depth offset (stacked clothing layers added up their glows of 0.02 into a white veil); fully transparent faces: no glow (LL classes them PASS_ALPHA_INVISIBLE, never drawn)
- ✅ Glow of stacked layers (mesh body, clothing): a face with glow whose texture has an alpha channel goes into LL's alpha pool (getPoolTypeFromTE / canRenderAsMask) and first erases the glow under it; before, the glows of 0.02 of each layer added up into an orange veil. Diagnostic visualisation: AURORA_DEBUG_GLOW=1 (AURORA_GLOW_SKIP=2/4/8 removes a family of faces). Coplanar opaque layers of mesh bodies (about ten at 0.02): maximum instead of the sum (the diagnostic showed ~0.25 on the skin) — accepted difference from the LL code; test cube (glow 0.2 fullbright) measured: Aurora halo ≈ Firestorm (a little shorter)
- ✅ SMAA by default for a new profile (`antialiasing: 6`), like the High to Ultra levels of Firestorm's featuretable (`RenderFSAAType 2`), instead of MSAA 4x. Existing profiles keep the saved setting, MSAA 4x included if it had never been changed; the renderer's starting state (`RenderSettings::default`) stays MSAA 4x, replaced by the viewer's setting before the first frame. Covered by a unit test (new profile, saved choice kept)
- ✅ Glow: a face seen from behind no longer adds anything (LLDrawPoolGlow and the emissives of LLDrawPoolAlpha keep the usual culling); before, the back face of a transparent shell (hair, fur, layer, aura) added its glow through the front face without being attenuated by it.
